\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{\_\_kprobe\_event\_add\_fields()}{\_\_kprobe\_event\_add\_fields()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} \+\_\+\+\_\+kprobe\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+fields (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structdynevent__cmd}{dynevent\+\_\+cmd}} $\ast$}]{cmd,  }\item[{}]{... }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

\+\_\+\+\_\+kprobe\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+fields -\/ Add probe fields to a kprobe command from arg list @cmd\+: A pointer to the \mbox{\hyperlink{structdynevent__cmd}{dynevent\+\_\+cmd}} struct representing the new event @args\+: Variable number of arg (pairs), one pair for each field

N\+O\+TE\+: Users normally won\textquotesingle{}t want to call this function directly, but rather use the \mbox{\hyperlink{linux_2trace__events_8h_a3ba9c7f76cbbd6b0cc724d24b2e107c3}{kprobe\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+fields()}} wrapper, which automatically adds a N\+U\+LL to the end of the arg list. If this function is used directly, make sure the last arg in the variable arg list is N\+U\+LL.

Add probe fields to an existing kprobe command using a variable list of args. Fields are added in the same order they\textquotesingle{}re listed.

Return\+: 0 if successful, error otherwise. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

register\+\_\+trace\+\_\+event -\/ register output for an event type @event\+: the event type to register

Event types are stored in a hash and this hash is used to find a way to print an event. If the @event-\/$>$type is set then it will use that type, otherwise it will assign a type to use.

If you assign your own type, please make sure it is added to the trace\+\_\+type enum in trace.\+h, to avoid collisions with the dynamic types.

Returns the event type number or zero on error. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+field\+\_\+str -\/ Add a new field to a synthetic event cmd @cmd\+: A pointer to the \mbox{\hyperlink{structdynevent__cmd}{dynevent\+\_\+cmd}} struct representing the new event @type\+\_\+name\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} type and name of the new field to add, as a single string

Add a new field to a synthetic event cmd object, as a single string. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} @type\+\_\+name string is expected to be of the form \textquotesingle{}type name\textquotesingle{}, which will be appended by \textquotesingle{};\textquotesingle{}. No sanity checking is done -\/ what\textquotesingle{}s passed in is assumed to already be well-\/formed. Field ordering is in the same order the fields are added.

See \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_aa60b769aa60096942c54ad33ee2fff01}{synth\+\_\+field\+\_\+size()}} for available types. If field\+\_\+name \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolcontains-p}{contains}} \mbox{[}n\mbox{]} the field is considered to be an array.

Return\+: 0 if successful, error otherwise. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+fields -\/ Add multiple fields to a synthetic event cmd @cmd\+: A pointer to the \mbox{\hyperlink{structdynevent__cmd}{dynevent\+\_\+cmd}} struct representing the new event @fields\+: An array of type/name field descriptions @n\+\_\+fields\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} number of field descriptions contained in the fields array

Add a new set of fields to a synthetic event cmd object. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} event fields that will be defined for the event should be passed in as an array of struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsynth__field__desc}{synth\+\_\+field\+\_\+desc}}, and the number of elements in the array passed in as n\+\_\+fields. Field ordering will retain the ordering given in the fields array.

See \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_aa60b769aa60096942c54ad33ee2fff01}{synth\+\_\+field\+\_\+size()}} for available types. If field\+\_\+name \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolcontains-p}{contains}} \mbox{[}n\mbox{]} the field is considered to be an array.

\index{synth\_event\_add\_next\_val@{synth\_event\_add\_next\_val}!trace\_events.h@{trace\_events.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{synth\_event\_add\_next\_val()}{synth\_event\_add\_next\_val()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+next\+\_\+val (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{actypes_8h_ae9add973f3ccbe0212adbfea3b68be67}{u64}}}]{val,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsynth__event__trace__state}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+state}} $\ast$}]{trace\+\_\+state }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+next\+\_\+val -\/ Add the next field\textquotesingle{}s value to an open synth trace @val\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} value to set the next field to @trace\+\_\+state\+: A pointer to object tracking the piecewise trace state

Set the value of the next field in an event that\textquotesingle{}s been opened by \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_aa5125e9f67b3162031aaf971eb595dcf}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+start()}}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} val param should be the value cast to u64. If the value points to a string, the val param should be a char $\ast$ cast to u64.

This function assumes all the fields in an event are to be set one after another -\/ successive calls to this function are made, one for each field, in the order of the fields in the event, until all fields have been set. If you\textquotesingle{}d rather set each field individually without regard to ordering, \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a9ee809ba78989acd350268e031f47fa8}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+val()}} can be used instead.

Note however that \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a7ed64017219e5e8272b6c4e4d2181c75}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+next\+\_\+val()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a9ee809ba78989acd350268e031f47fa8}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+val()}} can\textquotesingle{}t be intermixed for a given event trace -\/ one or the other but not both can be used at the same time.

Note also that \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a6cf41616e1640e14541c5ece5df3d5d0}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+end()}} must be called after all values have been added for each event trace, regardless of whether adding all field values succeeded or not.

Return\+: 0 on success, err otherwise. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{synth\_event\_add\_val()}{synth\_event\_add\_val()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+val (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const char $\ast$}]{field\+\_\+name,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{actypes_8h_ae9add973f3ccbe0212adbfea3b68be67}{u64}}}]{val,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsynth__event__trace__state}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+state}} $\ast$}]{trace\+\_\+state }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+val -\/ Add a named field\textquotesingle{}s value to an open synth trace @field\+\_\+name\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} name of the synthetic event field value to set @val\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} value to set the next field to @trace\+\_\+state\+: A pointer to object tracking the piecewise trace state

Set the value of the named field in an event that\textquotesingle{}s been opened by \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_aa5125e9f67b3162031aaf971eb595dcf}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+start()}}.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} val param should be the value cast to u64. If the value points to a string, the val param should be a char $\ast$ cast to u64.

This function looks up the field name, and if found, sets the field to the specified value. This lookup makes this function more expensive than \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a7ed64017219e5e8272b6c4e4d2181c75}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+next\+\_\+val()}}, so use that or the none-\/piecewise \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a0e5e77cc3abc8d9195faaaad6150b5b3}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace()}} instead if efficiency is more important.

Note however that \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a7ed64017219e5e8272b6c4e4d2181c75}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+next\+\_\+val()}} and \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a9ee809ba78989acd350268e031f47fa8}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+add\+\_\+val()}} can\textquotesingle{}t be intermixed for a given event trace -\/ one or the other but not both can be used at the same time.

Note also that \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_a6cf41616e1640e14541c5ece5df3d5d0}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+end()}} must be called after all values have been added for each event trace, regardless of whether adding all field values succeeded or not.

Return\+: 0 on success, err otherwise. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+end (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structsynth__event__trace__state}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+state}} $\ast$}]{trace\+\_\+state }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+end -\/ End piecewise synthetic event trace @trace\+\_\+state\+: A pointer to object tracking the piecewise trace state

End the trace of a synthetic event opened by synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+\+\_\+start().

This function \textquotesingle{}closes\textquotesingle{} an event trace, which basically means that it commits the reserved event and cleans up other loose ends.

A pointer to a trace\+\_\+state object is passed in, which will keep track of the current event trace state opened with \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events__synth_8c_aa5125e9f67b3162031aaf971eb595dcf}{synth\+\_\+event\+\_\+trace\+\_\+start()}}.

Note that this function must be called after all values have been added for each event trace, regardless of whether adding all field values succeeded or not.

Return\+: 0 on success, err otherwise. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{trace\_get\_event\_file()}{trace\_get\_event\_file()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structtrace__event__file}{trace\+\_\+event\+\_\+file}}$\ast$ trace\+\_\+get\+\_\+event\+\_\+file (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const char $\ast$}]{instance,  }\item[{const char $\ast$}]{system,  }\item[{const char $\ast$}]{event }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

trace\+\_\+get\+\_\+event\+\_\+file -\/ Find and return a trace event file @instance\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} name of the trace instance containing the event @system\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} name of the system containing the event @event\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} name of the event

Return a trace event file given the trace instance name, trace system, and trace event name. If the instance name is N\+U\+LL, it refers to the top-\/level trace array.

This function will look it up and return it if found, after calling \mbox{\hyperlink{kernel_2trace_2trace_8c_a7337fc205a21bb3b17265772efa6fa62}{trace\+\_\+array\+\_\+get()}} to prevent the instance from going away, and increment the event\textquotesingle{}s module refcount to prevent it from being removed.

To release the file, call \mbox{\hyperlink{trace__events_8c_a9c0d4f396c5663ce47b74703e08db465}{trace\+\_\+put\+\_\+event\+\_\+file()}}, which will call \mbox{\hyperlink{kernel_2trace_2trace_8c_ae2934950a9c7bd99e5ed10e599e9ee84}{trace\+\_\+array\+\_\+put()}} and decrement the event\textquotesingle{}s module refcount.

Return\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} trace event on success, E\+R\+R\+\_\+\+P\+TR otherwise. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 66
